14	export function runJunglePass(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, gs: GenState): number {

22	  const applyRandomMovement = (xRange: number, yRange: number) => {
23	    x += rng.int(Math.floor(-xRange * scale), Math.floor(xRange * scale));
24	    y += rng.int(Math.floor(-yRange * scale), Math.floor(yRange * scale));
25	    y = Math.max(gs.rockLevel, Math.min(h, y)); // 原版 clamp 上界 = maxTilesY（H 非 H-1）

29	    const str = rng.int(Math.floor(250 * scale), Math.floor(500 * scale) - 1);

31	    const stp = rng.int(50, 149);
32	    tileRunner(st, rng, gs, x, y, { strength: str, steps: stp, type: MUD, speedX: gs.dungeonSide * xSpeedScale, speedY: 0, mudWall: true });
33	  };
34	  const placeGemsAt = (baseGem: number, variants: number) => {
35	    // 原版 Next(-(int)(125*ws), (int)(125*ws)) 上界开 → int(a, b-1)。

38	    for (let n = 0; n < 6 * scale; n++) {
39	      tileRunner(st, rng, gs,
40	        x + rng.int(-Math.floor(125 * scale), Math.floor(125 * scale) - 1),
41	        y + rng.int(-Math.floor(125 * scale), Math.floor(125 * scale) - 1), {
42	          strength: rng.int(3, 6), steps: rng.int(3, 7),

47	
48	  applyRandomMovement(100, 100);
49	  zeroX += x; zeroY += y;
50	  placeFirstPassMud(3);
51	  placeGemsAt(0, 2);
52	  applyRandomMovement(250, 150);
53	  zeroX += x; zeroY += y;

65	  const num2 = rng.int(Math.floor(400 * scale), Math.floor(600 * scale) - 1);
66	  const num3 = Math.floor(25 * scale);
67	  const i = Math.max(gs.beachLeftEnd + Math.floor(num2 / 2) + num3, Math.min(gs.beachRightStart - Math.floor(num2 / 2) - num3, cx));

70	  tileRunner(st, rng, gs, i, cy, { strength: num2, steps: 10000, type: MUD, speedY: -20, noYChange: true, mudWall: true });
71	  // 泥柱中心记录给后续(丛林表面草化)
72	  (gs as any).jungleCoreX = i;

74	  generateHolesInMudWalls(st, rng, gs);
75	  generateFinishingTouches(st, rng, gs, oldX, oldY, scale, van);
76	  return jungleXOut;
77	}

79	function generateTunnelToSurface(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, gs: GenState, i: number, j: number): number {
80	  let radius = rng.int(5, 10);
81	  let px = i + 0.0, py = j + 0.0;
82	  let vx = rng.int(-10, 10) * 0.1;
83	  let vy = rng.int(10, 19) * 0.1;
84	  let count = 0;
85	  let running = true;
86	  let jungleX = i;
87	  while (running) {
88	    if (py < gs.worldSurface) {
89	      const cx = Math.max(10, Math.min(st.w - 10, Math.floor(px)));
90	      const cy = Math.max(10, Math.min(st.h - 10, Math.floor(py)));
91	      // 隧道顶到达无墙无块的开放区即停(检查上方 1-5 格)
92	      let open = true;
93	      for (let d = 0; d <= 5; d++) {
94	        const ii = st.idx(cx, Math.max(5, cy - d));
95	        if (st.wall[ii] !== 0 || st.flags[ii]) { open = false; break; }
96	      }
97	      if (open) running = false;
98	    }
99	    jungleX = Math.floor(px);
100	    radius += rng.int(-20, 20) * 0.1;
101	    if (radius < 5) radius = 5;
102	    if (radius > 10) radius = 10;
103	    const x0 = Math.max(10, Math.min(st.w - 10, Math.floor(px - radius * 0.5)));
104	    const x1 = Math.max(10, Math.min(st.w - 10, Math.floor(px + radius * 0.5)));
105	    const y0 = Math.max(10, Math.min(st.h - 10, Math.floor(py - radius * 0.5)));
106	    const y1 = Math.max(10, Math.min(st.h - 10, Math.floor(py + radius * 0.5)));
107	    for (let x = x0; x < x1; x++) {
108	      for (let y = y0; y < y1; y++) {
109	        if (Math.abs(x - px) + Math.abs(y - py) < radius * 0.5 * (1 + rng.int(-10, 10) * 0.015)) {
110	          // 原版 KillTile：只 active(false) **保留 type**（幽灵语义）——清零会
111	          // 让后续拒绝采样（查 type==59）判定分叉
112	          st.flags[st.idx(x, y)] = 0;
113	        }
114	      }
115	    }
116	    count++;
117	    // 偶发分支洞穴
118	    if (count > 10 && rng.int(0, 49) < count) {
119	      count = 0;
120	      const side = rng.next() < 0.5 ? 2 : -2;   // 原版 num4 默认 -2、Next(2)==0 才 +2(JunglePass.cs:178-182),曾写反
121	      tileRunner(st, rng, gs, Math.floor(px), Math.floor(py), {
122	        strength: rng.int(3, 19), steps: rng.int(10, 99), type: -1, speedX: side,
123	      });
124	    }
125	    px += vx; py += vy;
126	    vy += rng.int(-10, 10) * 0.01;
127	    if (vy > 0) vy = 0;
128	    if (vy < -2) vy = -2;
129	    vx += rng.int(-10, 10) * 0.1;
130	    if (px < i - 200) vx += rng.int(5, 20) * 0.1;
131	    if (px > i + 200) vx -= rng.int(5, 20) * 0.1;
132	    if (vx > 1.5) vx = 1.5;
133	    if (vx < -1.5) vx = -1.5;
134	    if (py < -50 || py > st.h + 50) running = false; // 安全阀
135	  }
136	  return jungleX;
137	}
138	
139	/** 泥墙挖洞（cs:12503-12520 + MudWallRunner cs:77584-77663 1:1）：
140	 *  w/4 次：拒绝采样到墙 64/15 格后跑 MudWallRunner（TileRunner 形状的清墙游走）。
141	 *  曾是"简化随机走"自创实现——掷骰序列完全不同 */
142	function generateHolesInMudWalls(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, gs: GenState): void {
143	  const { w, h } = st;
144	  const underworld = h - 200; // Main.UnderworldLayer（Main.cs:2863）
145	  const tries = Math.floor(w / 4);
146	  for (let n = 0; n < tries; n++) {
147	    let x = rng.int(20, w - 21);
148	    let y = rng.int(gs.worldSurface + 10, underworld - 1); // Next(wS+10, UnderworldLayer)
149	    while (!(st.wall[st.idx(x, y)] === 64 || st.wall[st.idx(x, y)] === 15)) {
150	      x = rng.int(20, w - 21);
151	      y = rng.int(gs.worldSurface + 10, underworld - 1);
152	    }
153	    mudWallRunner(st, rng, gs, x, y);
154	  }
155	}
156	
157	/** MudWallRunner（cs:77584）：衰减半径游走，菱形域内（y>worldSurface）清墙 */
158	function mudWallRunner(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, gs: GenState, i: number, j: number): void {
159	  const { w, h } = st;
160	  const num = rng.int(8, 20) + 0.0;          // Next(8,21)（double）
161	  const num2 = rng.int(8, 32) + 0.0;         // Next(8,33)（double）
162	  let num3 = num2;
163	  let px = i + 0.0, py = j + 0.0;
164	  let vx = rng.int(-10, 10) * 0.1, vy = rng.int(-10, 10) * 0.1;
165	  while (num > 0 && num3 > 0) {
166	    const num4 = num * (num3 / num2);
167	    num3 -= 1;
168	    let x0 = Math.trunc(px - num4 * 0.5), x1 = Math.trunc(px + num4 * 0.5);
169	    let y0 = Math.trunc(py - num4 * 0.5), y1 = Math.trunc(py + num4 * 0.5);
170	    if (x0 < 0) x0 = 0;
171	    if (x1 > w) x1 = w;
172	    if (y0 < 0) y0 = 0;
173	    if (y1 > h) y1 = h;
174	    for (let x = x0; x < x1; x++) {
175	      for (let y = y0; y < y1; y++) {
176	        if (Math.abs(x - px) + Math.abs(y - py) < num * 0.5 * (1 + rng.int(-10, 10) * 0.015)
177	          && y > gs.worldSurface) {
178	          st.wall[st.idx(x, y)] = 0;
179	        }
180	      }
181	    }
182	    px += vx; py += vy;
183	    vx += rng.int(-10, 10) * 0.05;
184	    if (vx > 1) vx = 1;
185	    if (vx < -1) vx = -1;
186	    vy += rng.int(-10, 10) * 0.05;
187	    if (vy > 1) vy = 1;
188	    if (vy < -1) vy = -1;
189	  }
190	}
191	
192	function generateFinishingTouches(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, gs: GenState, oldX: number, oldY: number, scale: number, van: number): void {
193	  void van;
194	  let x = oldX, y = oldY;
195	  // 大泥簇
196	  for (let n = 0; n <= 20 * scale; n++) {
197	    // 原版 Next((int)(-5*ws), (int)(6*ws))：上界 6 非 5（曾写对称 ±5）
198	    x += rng.int(-Math.floor(5 * scale), Math.floor(6 * scale) - 1);
199	    y += rng.int(-Math.floor(5 * scale), Math.floor(6 * scale) - 1);
200	    // 原版 300-500 步固定不缩放
201	    const str = rng.int(40, 99), stp = rng.int(300, 499);
202	    tileRunner(st, rng, gs, x, y, { strength: str, steps: stp, type: MUD });
203	  }
204	  // 洞穴(1/7 填液体)
205	  for (let n = 0; n <= 10 * scale; n++) {
206	    // Next(-A, A) 上界开 → int(-A, A-1)；拒绝采样无界 while（原版，无 40 次上限）
207	    let i2 = oldX + rng.int(-Math.floor(600 * scale), Math.floor(600 * scale) - 1);
208	    let j2 = oldY + rng.int(-Math.floor(200 * scale), Math.floor(200 * scale) - 1);
209	    while (!(i2 >= 1 && i2 < st.w - 1 && j2 >= 1 && j2 < st.h - 1 && st.type[st.idx(i2, j2)] === MUD)) {
210	      i2 = oldX + rng.int(-Math.floor(600 * scale), Math.floor(600 * scale) - 1);
211	      j2 = oldY + rng.int(-Math.floor(200 * scale), Math.floor(200 * scale) - 1);
212	    }
213	    for (let k = 0; k < 8 * scale; k++) {
214	      i2 += rng.int(-30, 30);
215	      j2 += rng.int(-30, 30);
216	      // ★原版掷序（cs:245-247）：type(Next 7) → strength(Next 10,20) → steps(Next 30,70)
217	      //（对象字面量曾按 strength/steps/type 求值 = 流错位）
218	      const cavType = rng.next() * 7 < 1 ? -2 : -1;
219	      const cavStr = rng.int(10, 19), cavStp = rng.int(30, 69);
220	      tileRunner(st, rng, gs, i2, j2, { strength: cavStr, steps: cavStp, type: cavType });
221	    }
222	  }
223	  // 泥内石囊 + 宝石
224	  for (let n = 0; n <= 300 * scale; n++) {
225	    let i2 = oldX + rng.int(-Math.floor(600 * scale), Math.floor(600 * scale) - 1);
226	    let j2 = oldY + rng.int(-Math.floor(200 * scale), Math.floor(200 * scale) - 1);
227	    while (!(i2 >= 1 && i2 < st.w - 1 && j2 >= 1 && j2 < st.h - 1 && st.type[st.idx(i2, j2)] === MUD)) {
228	      i2 = oldX + rng.int(-Math.floor(600 * scale), Math.floor(600 * scale) - 1);
229	      j2 = oldY + rng.int(-Math.floor(200 * scale), Math.floor(200 * scale) - 1);
230	    }
231	    tileRunner(st, rng, gs, i2, j2, { strength: rng.int(4, 9), steps: rng.int(5, 29), type: T.STONE });
232	    if (rng.next() * 4 < 1) {
233	      // ★原版掷序（cs:269-271）：type2(Next 63,69) 在实参求值**之前**求值 →
234	      // type → x偏移 → y偏移 → strength → steps（对象字面量会把 type 排到最后=流错位）
235	      const gtype = GEMS[rng.int(0, 5)];
236	      tileRunner(st, rng, gs,
237	        i2 + rng.int(-1, 1), j2 + rng.int(-1, 1),
238	        { strength: rng.int(3, 6), steps: rng.int(4, 7), type: gtype });
239	    }
240	  }
241	}
242	

```

106	export function runMicroBiomesPass(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, gs: GenState): void {
107	  // Skyblock.denyAllGeneration 包裹整个 pass(cs:21787)——1.4.5.6 skyblock 种子全段跳过
108	  if (gs.seedFlags.skyblock) return;
109	  const { w, h } = st;
110	  const protectedRects: Rect[] = [];
111	  // WorldGenRange ScalingMode(WorldGenRange.cs:43-58):
112	  //   WorldArea = w*h/5040000;WorldWidth = w/4200;ScaleValue=(int)截断
113	  const areaScale = (w * h) / 5040000;
114	  const widthScale = w / 4200;
115	  const scW = (v: number): number => Math.trunc(widthScale * v);
116	
117	  // ---- 1. Dead Man's Chests(cs:21801-21919 段 21801-21819)----
118	  {
119	    const deadMans = new DeadMansChestBiome(st, rng, gs);
120	    // GetPossibleChestsToTrapify 先于计数掷骰(cs:21806-21807)——每候选 4 掷
121	    const possible = deadMans.getPossibleChests();
122	    const deadCount = rng.int(scW(10), scW(20));   // DeadManChests 10-20 ×WorldWidth
123	    let done = 0;
124	    let guard = 3000;                              // num4
125	    while (done < deadCount && possible.length > 0) {
126	      if (--guard <= 0) break;
127	      const pick = rng.int(0, possible.length - 1);
128	      const c = possible[pick];
129	      deadMans.place(c.x, c.y);                    // 返回值原版丢弃(cs:21814)
130	      done++;
131	      possible.splice(pick, 1);
132	    }
133	  }
134	
135	  // ---- 2. Thin Ice(cs:21825-21854)----
136	  if (!gs.seedFlags.notTheBees || gs.seedFlags.remix) {
137	    const iceCount = rng.int(scW(3), scW(5));      // ThinIcePatchCount 3-5 ×WorldWidth
138	    let iceDone = 0, iceFail = 0;
139	    const iceFailLimit = 1000;                     // num9
140	    while (iceDone < iceCount) {
141	      // RandomWorldPoint(worldSurface+20, 50, 200, 50)(cs:21836)
142	      const ox = rng.int(50, w - 51);
143	      const oy = rng.int(Math.floor(gs.worldSurface) + 20, h - 201);
144	      if (placeThinIcePatch(st, rng, protectedRects, ox, oy)) {
145	        iceDone++;
146	        iceFail = 0;
147	      } else {
148	        iceFail++;
149	        if (iceFail > iceFailLimit) { iceDone++; iceFail = 0; }   // cs:21848-21852 强制推进
150	      }
151	    }
152	  }
153	
154	  // ---- 3. Campsites(cs:21915-21936,最多 1000 次尝试;cs:21914 notTheBees 门)----
155	  const campOk = !gs.seedFlags.notTheBees || gs.seedFlags.remix;
156	  const campCount = campOk
157	    ? rng.int(Math.max(1, Math.trunc(areaScale * 6)), Math.max(1, Math.trunc(areaScale * 11)))
158	    : 0;
159	  let placed = 0, tries = 0;
160	  while (placed < campCount) {
161	    if (++tries > 1000) break;
162	    const ox = rng.int(gs.beachDistance, w - gs.beachDistance - 1); // RandomWorldPoint(top,right,bottom,left)
163	    const oy = rng.int(Math.floor(gs.worldSurface), h - 201);
164	    if (placeCampsite(st, rng, protectedRects, ox, oy)) placed++;
165	  }
166	
167	  // ---- 4. Mining Explosives(cs:21951-21975,最多 3000 次尝试;cs:21956 notTheBees 门)----
168	  const trapOk = campOk;
169	  let trapCount = trapOk
170	    ? rng.int(Math.max(1, Math.trunc(areaScale * 14)), Math.max(1, Math.trunc(areaScale * 29)))
171	    : 0;
172	  if (trapOk && (gs.seedFlags.getGoodWorld || gs.seedFlags.noTraps) && !gs.seedFlags.notTheBees) {
173	    trapCount = Math.trunc(trapCount * 1.5);       // cs:21958-21961
174	  }
175	  placed = 0; tries = 0;
176	  while (placed < trapCount) {
177	    if (++tries > 3000) break;
178	    const ox = rng.int(gs.beachDistance, w - gs.beachDistance - 1);
179	    const oy = rng.int(Math.floor(gs.rockLevel), h - 201);
180	    if (placeMiningExplosives(st, rng, gs, protectedRects, ox, oy)) placed++;
181	  }
182	
183	  // ---- 5. TrackGenerator(cs:22014-22091)----
184	  const trackGen = new TrackGenerator(st, gs);
185	  const trackFailLimit = Math.floor(w / 2);        // num25
186	  // 长轨:LongTrackCount 1-2 ×WorldWidth,长 400-1000 ×WorldWidth
187	  let trackCount = rng.int(scW(1), scW(2));
188	  let tDone = 0, tFail = 0;
189	  while (tDone < trackCount) {
190	    const ox = rng.int(10, w - 11);                // RandomWorldPoint(worldSurface, 10, 200, 10)
191	    const oy = rng.int(Math.floor(gs.worldSurface), h - 201);
192	    if (trackGen.place(ox, oy, scW(400), scW(1000), rng)) {
193	      tDone++; tFail = 0;
194	    } else {
195	      tFail++;
196	      if (tFail > trackFailLimit) { tDone++; tFail = 0; }
197	    }
198	  }
199	  // 标准轨:StandardTrackCount 4-7 ×WorldArea,长 150-300 ×WorldWidth
200	  trackCount = rng.int(Math.max(1, Math.trunc(areaScale * 4)), Math.max(1, Math.trunc(areaScale * 7)));
201	  tDone = 0; tFail = 0;
202	  while (tDone < trackCount) {
203	    const ox = rng.int(10, w - 11);
204	    const oy = rng.int(Math.floor(gs.worldSurface), h - 201);
205	    if (trackGen.place(ox, oy, scW(150), scW(300), rng)) {
206	      tDone++; tFail = 0;
207	    } else {
208	      tFail++;
209	      if (tFail > trackFailLimit) { tDone++; tFail = 0; }
210	    }
211	  }
212	
213	  // ---- 6. placeLavaTrap(cs:22093-22127):w*0.02 次 × 10150 尝试 ----
214	  if (!gs.seedFlags.notTheBees) {
215	    const lavaTries = w * 0.02;                    // num31(裸 double 比较,不取整)
216	    for (let j = 0; j < lavaTries; j++) {
217	      for (let k = 0; k < 10150; k++) {
218	        const x = rng.int(200, w - 201);
219	        const y = rng.int(gs.lavaLine - 100, h - 211);   // Next(lavaLine-100, h-210)
220	        if (placeLavaTrap(st, gs, x, y)) break;
221	      }
222	    }
223	  }
224	}
225	
226	/** CampsiteBiome.Place(CampsiteBiome.cs:9-116) */
227	function placeCampsite(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, protectedRects: Rect[], ox: number, oy: number): boolean {
228	  // Circle(10):实心占比门禁(solid ≥ total-5,cs:12-17)
229	  {
230	    let total = 0, solid = 0;
231	    const r = 10, rr = (r + 1) * (r + 1);
232	    for (let dy = -r; dy <= r; dy++) {
233	      const half = Math.min(r, Math.trunc(Math.sqrt(rr - dy * dy)));
234	      for (let dx = -half; dx <= half; dx++) {
235	        total++;
236	        if (solidAt(st, ox + dx, oy + dy)) solid++;
237	      }
238	    }
239	    if (solid < total - 5) return false;
240	  }
241	  const num = rng.int(6, 9);  // Next(6,10)
242	  const num2 = rng.int(1, 4); // Next(1,5)
243	  const rect: Rect = { x0: ox - num, y0: oy - num, x1: ox + num + 1, y1: oy + num + 1 };
244	  if (protectedRects.some((p) => overlaps(rect, p))) return false;
245	  // (num+3)² 内禁地牢砖/容器/神庙砖/祭坛(cs:24-35)
246	  {
247	    const n3 = num + 3;
248	    for (let dx = -n3; dx <= n3; dx++) {
249	      for (let dy = -n3; dy <= n3; dy++) {
250	        if (!st.inBounds(ox + dx, oy + dy)) continue;
251	        const i = st.idx(ox + dx, oy + dy);
252	        if (!st.flags[i]) continue;
253	        const t = st.type[i];
254	        if (t === LIHARD || t === LIHARD_ALTAR) return false;
255	        // Main.tileDungeon 近似:三种地牢砖(41/43/44)
256	        if (t === DUNGEON_BLUE || t === DUNGEON_GREEN || t === DUNGEON_PINK) return false;
257	        const key = TILE_DEFS[t]?.key ?? '';
258	        if (key.includes('chest') || key.includes('dresser')) return false; // IsAContainer 近似
259	      }
260	    }
261	  }
262	  // 墙类型按地表材质(cs:36-66)
263	  let wallType = 196 + rng.int(0, 3);
264	  for (let dx = -num; dx <= num; dx++) {
265	    for (let dy = -num; dy <= num; dy++) {
266	      if (!st.inBounds(ox + dx, oy + dy)) continue;
267	      const i = st.idx(ox + dx, oy + dy);
268	      if (!st.flags[i]) continue;
269	      const t = st.type[i];
270	      if (t === SAND || t === SANDSTONE || t === HARDSAND || t === FOSSIL) wallType = 171;
271	      else if (t === SNOW || t === ICE) wallType = 40;
272	      else if (t === JUNGLE_GRASS) wallType = 204 + rng.int(0, 3);
273	      else if (t === MARBLE) wallType = 178;
274	      else if (t === GRANITE) wallType = 180;
275	    }
276	  }
277	  // Slime(num) + Blotches(num2,num2,num2,1,chance 1.0) 形状(cs:67-68):
278	  //  blotch 点入 ShapeData;Offset(0,-2) 后沙 53→397;空墙 → wallType
279	  const data = new Set<number>();
280	  const r2 = (num + 1) * (num + 1);
281	  const slimeRows: Array<[number, number]> = [];
282	  for (let dy = -num; dy <= 0; dy++) {
283	    slimeRows.push([dy, Math.min(num, Math.trunc(Math.sqrt(r2 - dy * dy)))]);
284	  }
285	  for (let k = 1; k <= Math.trunc(num * 0.5) - 1; k++) {
286	    slimeRows.push([k, Math.min(num, Math.trunc(Math.sqrt(r2 - (k * 2) ** 2)))]);
287	  }
288	  for (const [dy, half] of slimeRows) {
289	    for (let dx = -half; dx <= half; dx++) {
290	      // Blotches(Apply:先掷 1 次 NextDouble 丢弃,再判 chance=1.0;矩形 x[1-n2,n2-1] y[1-n2,0])
291	      rng.next();
292	      rng.next();
293	      const bx0 = rng.int(1 - num2, 0), bx1 = rng.int(0, num2 - 1);
294	      const by0 = rng.int(1 - num2, 0), by1 = 0;
295	      for (let bi = bx0; bi <= bx1; bi++) {
296	        for (let bj = by0; bj <= by1; bj++) {
297	          const px = ox + dx + bi, py = oy + dy + bj;
298	          data.add(py * st.w + px);
299	          if (!st.inBounds(px, py)) continue;
300	          const up = py - 2; // Modifiers.Offset(0,-2)
301	          if (st.inBounds(px, up)) {
302	            const ui = st.idx(px, up);
303	            if (st.flags[ui] && st.type[ui] === SAND) st.type[ui] = HARDSAND_TILE;
304	          }
305	          const pi = st.idx(px, py);
306	          if (st.wall[pi] === 0) st.wall[pi] = wallType;
307	        }
308	      }
309	    }
310	  }
311	  // All(data):ClearTile + SetLiquid(0,0) + 空墙刷墙(cs:69)
312	  for (const key of data) {
313	    const px = key % st.w, py = Math.trunc(key / st.w);
314	    if (!st.inBounds(px, py)) continue;
315	    const i = st.idx(px, py);
316	    st.flags[i] = 0;
317	    st.liquid[i] = 0; st.liquidType[i] = 0;
318	    if (st.wall[i] === 0) st.wall[i] = wallType;
319	  }
320	  // 向下 10 格找地面(cs:70-74)
321	  let groundY = oy;
322	  {
323	    let found = false;
324	    for (let k = 0; k < 10 && !found; k++) {
325	      if (solidAt(st, ox, oy + k)) { groundY = oy + k; found = true; }
326	    }
327	    if (!found) return false;
328	  }
329	  const num4 = groundY - 1;
330	  const flag = rng.next() < 0.5; // Next() % 2 == 0
331	  // 金币堆帐篷(cs:76-88,1/10 跳过)
332	  if (Math.trunc(rng.next() * 10) !== 0) {
333	    const num5 = rng.int(1, 3); // Next(1,4)
334	    const num6 = flag ? 4 : -Math.trunc(num / 2);
335	    for (let m = 0; m < num5; m++) {
336	      const num7 = rng.int(1, 2); // Next(1,3)
337	      for (let n = 0; n < num7; n++) {
338	        const px = ox + num6 - m, py = num4 - n;
339	        if (!st.inBounds(px, py)) continue;
340	        const i = st.idx(px, py);
341	        if (!st.flags[i] && solidAt(st, px, py + 1)) st.setTileSilent(px, py, COIN_PILE, 0, 0);
342	      }
343	    }
344	  }
345	  // 倒木 186(cs:89-93,1/10 跳过)
346	  const num8 = (num - 3) * (flag ? -1 : 1);
347	  if (Math.trunc(rng.next() * 10) !== 0) {
348	    const px = ox + num8;
349	    placeLargePile(st, px, num4);
350	  }
351	  // 篝火 215(cs:94-113,1/10 跳过;frameY+36 = 未点燃行)
352	  if (Math.trunc(rng.next() * 10) !== 0) {
353	    let ok = true;
354	    for (let dx = 0; dx < 3 && ok; dx++) {
355	      for (let dy = -1; dy <= 0; dy++) {
356	        if (!st.inBounds(ox + dx, num4 + dy) || st.flags[st.idx(ox + dx, num4 + dy)]) ok = false;
357	      }
358	    }
359	    if (ok) {
360	      for (let dx = 0; dx < 3; dx++) {
361	        for (let dy = -1; dy <= 0; dy++) {
362	          st.setTileSilent(ox + dx, num4 + dy, CAMPFIRE, dx * 18, dy * 18 + 36);
363	        }
364	      }
365	    }
366	  }
367	  protectedRects.push(rect);
368	  return true;
369	}
370	
371	/** tile 186 LargePiles 3×2(style 0 帧,PlaceTile 默认 style)。
372	 *  原版经 PlaceTile → case 186 的 SolidTileAllowBottomSlope 门（WorldGen.cs:49284）：
373	 *  地面须实心且非半砖非顶坡——斜坡格上不生成（与 pilesPass 同门） */
374	function placeLargePile(st: TileStore, x: number, y: number): void {
375	  for (let dx = 0; dx < 3; dx++) {
376	    if (!st.inBounds(x + dx, y)) return;
377	    const gi = st.idx(x + dx, y);
378	    if (!solidAt(st, x + dx, y)
379	      || st.half[gi] === 1 || (st.slope[gi] >= 1 && st.slope[gi] <= 2)) return;
380	  }
381	  for (let dx = 0; dx < 3; dx++) {
382	    for (let dy = 0; dy < 2; dy++) {
383	      if (!st.inBounds(x + dx, y - 1 + dy)) continue;
384	      const i = st.idx(x + dx, y - 1 + dy);
385	      if (st.flags[i]) continue;
386	      st.setTileSilent(x + dx, y - 1 + dy, LARGE_PILES, dx * 18, dy * 18);
387	    }
388	  }
389	}
390	
391	/** MiningExplosivesBiome.Place(MiningExplosivesBiome.cs:10-76) */
392	function placeMiningExplosives(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, gs: GenState, protectedRects: Rect[], ox: number, oy: number): boolean {
393	  if (solidAt(st, ox, oy)) return false;
394	  {
395	    const wl = st.inBounds(ox, oy) ? st.wall[st.idx(ox, oy)] : -1;
396	    if (wl === 216 || wl === 187) return false; // 地下沙漠墙门禁(cs:17-20)
397	  }
398	  const barType = [gs.oreTiers.gold, gs.oreTiers.silver, gs.oreTiers.iron, gs.oreTiers.copper][rng.int(0, 3)];
399	  const dir = rng.next() * 2 - 1; // cs:22
400	  // Find(origin, Right(40)|Left(40) 直到实心) → origin(MiningExplosivesBiome.cs:23-26)
401	  let x = ox, y = oy;
402	  {
403	    const step = dir > 0 ? 1 : -1;
404	    let found = false;
405	    for (let k = 0; k < 40 && !found; k++) {
406	      x += step;
407	      if (solidAt(st, x, y)) found = true;
408	    }
409	    if (!found) return false;
410	  }
411	  // Find(origin, Down(80) 直到实心)(cs:27-30)
412	  {
413	    let found = false;
414	    for (let k = 0; k < 80 && !found; k++) {
415	      y++;
416	      if (solidAt(st, x, y)) found = true;
417	    }
418	    if (!found) return false;
419	  }
420	  // ShapeRunner(10, 20, (dir,1)) + Blotches(2, 0.3):矿石地板形状(cs:31-38)
421	  const data = new Set<number>();
422	  let total = 0, solid = 0;
423	  {
424	    let steps = 20.0, strength = 10.0;
425	    let px = x + 0.0, py = y + 0.0;
426	    let vx = dir + 0.0, vy = 1.0;
427	    while (steps > 0 && strength > 0) {
428	      strength = 10 * (steps / 20);
429	      steps -= 1;
430	      const bx0 = Math.max(1, Math.trunc(px - strength * 0.5)), by0 = Math.max(1, Math.trunc(py - strength * 0.5));
431	      const bx1 = Math.min(st.w, Math.trunc(px + strength * 0.5)), by1 = Math.min(st.h, Math.trunc(py + strength * 0.5));
432	      for (let i = bx0; i < bx1; i++) {
433	        for (let j = by0; j < by1; j++) {
434	          if (Math.abs(i - px) + Math.abs(j - py) >= strength * 0.5 * (1 + rng.int(-10, 10) * 0.015)) continue;
435	          // Blotches(2, 0.3)(Modifiers.cs:92-157)
436	          rng.next();
437	          const pts: Array<[number, number]> = [];
438	          if (rng.next() < 0.3) {
439	            const ox0 = rng.int(-1, 0), ox1 = rng.int(0, 1), oy0 = rng.int(-1, 0), oy1 = rng.int(0, 1);
440	            for (let bi = ox0; bi <= ox1; bi++) for (let bj = oy0; bj <= oy1; bj++) pts.push([i + bi, j + bj]);
441	          } else {
442	            pts.push([i, j]);
443	          }
444	          for (const [qx, qy] of pts) {
445	            data.add(qy * st.w + qx);
446	            total++;
447	            if (solidAt(st, qx, qy)) solid++;
448	          }
449	        }
450	      }
451	      const n8 = Math.trunc(strength / 50) + 1;
452	      steps -= n8;
453	      px += vx; py += vy;
454	      for (let k = 0; k < n8; k++) {
455	        px += vx; py += vy;
456	        vx += rng.range(-0.5, 0.5); vy += rng.range(-0.5, 0.5);
457	      }
458	      vx += rng.range(-0.5, 0.5); vy += rng.range(-0.5, 0.5);
459	      vx = Math.max(-1, Math.min(1, vx));
460	      vy = Math.max(-1, Math.min(1, vy));
461	    }
462	  }
463	  if (solid < Math.trunc(total / 2)) return false; // cs:35-38
464	  const area: Rect = { x0: x - 15, y0: y - 10, x1: x + 15, y1: y + 10 };
465	  if (protectedRects.some((p) => overlaps(area, p))) return false;
466	  // 矿石地板(cs:44)
467	  for (const key of data) {
468	    const qx = key % st.w, qy = Math.trunc(key / st.w);
469	    if (!st.inBounds(qx, qy)) continue;
470	    const i = st.idx(qx, qy);
471	    st.type[i] = barType; st.flags[i] = 1;
472	  }
473	  // Circle(5) + Blotches 挖房(cs:45):中心 = (origin.X - dir*-5, origin.Y - 5)
474	  {
475	    const cx = x - Math.trunc(-dir * 5), cy = y - 5;
476	    const r = 5, rr = (r + 1) * (r + 1);
477	    for (let dy = -r; dy <= r; dy++) {
478	      const half = Math.min(r, Math.trunc(Math.sqrt(rr - dy * dy)));
479	      for (let dx = -half; dx <= half; dx++) {
480	        rng.next();
481	        const pts: Array<[number, number]> = [];
482	        if (rng.next() < 0.3) {
483	          const ox0 = rng.int(-1, 0), ox1 = rng.int(0, 1), oy0 = rng.int(-1, 0), oy1 = rng.int(0, 1);
484	          for (let bi = ox0; bi <= ox1; bi++) for (let bj = oy0; bj <= oy1; bj++) pts.push([cx + dx + bi, cy + dy + bj]);
485	        } else {
486	          pts.push([cx + dx, cy + dy]);
487	        }
488	        for (const [qx, qy] of pts) {
489	          if (!st.inBounds(qx, qy)) continue;
490	          const i = st.idx(qx, qy);
491	          st.flags[i] = 0; // ClearTile(frameNeighbors)
492	        }
493	      }
494	    }
495	  }
496	  // 两侧落点向下 10 格找地面(cs:47-52)
497	  const findDown = (fx: number, fy: number): number => {
498	    for (let k = 0; k < 10; k++) {
499	      if (solidAt(st, fx, fy + k)) return fy + k;
500	    }
501	    return -1;
502	  };
503	  const barX = x - (dir > 0 ? 3 : -3);
504	  const num3 = rng.int(0, 3) === 0 ? 3 : 7; // cs:48
505	  const detX = x - (dir > 0 ? -num3 : num3);
506	  let barGround = findDown(barX, y - 3);
507	  const detGround = findDown(detX, y - 3);
508	  if (barGround < 0 || detGround < 0) return false;
509	  barGround--; // result.Y--
510	  const detTop = detGround - 1; // result2.Y--
511	  // 起爆器下方 3 列整平(cs:55-70)
512	  for (let i = -1; i <= 1; i++) {
513	    const px = detX + i, py = detTop;
514	    if (st.inBounds(px, py)) st.flags[st.idx(px, py)] = 0;
515	    const below = detTop + 1;
516	    if (st.inBounds(px, below) && !solidAt(st, px, below)) {
517	      st.type[st.idx(px, below)] = STONE; st.flags[st.idx(px, below)] = 1;
518	    }
519	  }
520	  // 炸药桶 141 + 起爆器 411(2×2)+ 红线直连(cs:71-73)
521	  if (st.inBounds(barX, barGround) && !st.flags[st.idx(barX, barGround)]) {
522	    st.setTileSilent(barX, barGround, EXPLOSIVES, 0, 0);
523	  }
524	  // 起爆器 411:PlaceTile(..., forced: true)(cs:72)——无视支撑/占位检查强制 2×2 落块
525	  for (let dx = 0; dx < 2; dx++) {
526	    for (let dy = 0; dy < 2; dy++) {
527	      if (!st.inBounds(detX + dx, detTop + dy)) continue;
528	      st.setTileSilent(detX + dx, detTop + dy, DETONATOR, dx * 18, dy * 18);
529	    }
530	  }
531	  // WorldUtils.WireLine(WorldUtils.cs:111-131):L 形红线
532	  {
533	    const sx = Math.min(barX, detX), ex = Math.max(barX, detX);
534	    const sy = Math.min(barGround, detTop), ey = Math.max(barGround, detTop);
535	    for (let i = sx; i <= ex; i++) {
536	      if (st.inBounds(i, barGround)) st.wire[st.idx(i, barGround)] |= WIRE_RED;
537	    }
538	    for (let j = sy; j <= ey; j++) {
539	      if (st.inBounds(detX, j)) st.wire[st.idx(detX, j)] |= WIRE_RED;
540	    }
541	  }
542	  protectedRects.push(area);
543	  return true;
544	}
545	
546	// ============================================================
547	// 1. DeadMansChestBiome(Terraria.GameContent.Biomes/DeadMansChestBiome.cs 全文)
548	// ============================================================
549	
550	interface DartSpot { x: number; y: number; directionX: number; xPush: number; trapX: number; trapY: number }
551	interface WireSpot { x: number; y: number; dirX: number; dirY: number; steps: number }
552	interface BoulderSpot { x: number; y: number; yPush: number; bestType: number }
553	
554	/** WorldGen.countWires(WorldGen.cs:9462):四色线逐位计数 */
555	function countWires(st: TileStore, x: number, y: number, size: number): number {
556	  let n = 0;
557	  for (let i = x - size; i <= x + size; i++) {
558	    for (let j = y - size; j <= y + size; j++) {
559	      if (!st.inBounds(i, j)) continue;
560	      const b = st.wire[st.idx(i, j)] & 15;
561	      n += (b & 1) + ((b >> 1) & 1) + ((b >> 2) & 1) + ((b >> 3) & 1);
562	    }
563	  }
564	  return n;
565	}
566	
567	/** WorldGen.countTiles(WorldGen.cs:9493 + nextCount:9506-9577,jungle=false/lavaOk=true):
568	 *  非实心格洪水填充计数。墙≠0/墙 244/微光液体/贴边 → 直接判满(3500);
569	 *  结果只与连通域相关,与遍历序无关(< 40 门槛与 3500 封顶均序不变) */
570	const MAX_TILE_COUNT = 3500;   // WorldGen.maxTileCount(cs:4194)
571	function countTiles(st: TileStore, x0: number, y0: number): number {
572	  let count = 0;
573	  const seen = new Set<number>();
574	  const stack: Array<[number, number]> = [[x0, y0]];
575	  while (stack.length > 0) {
576	    if (count >= MAX_TILE_COUNT) return count;
577	    const [x, y] = stack.pop()!;
578	    if (x <= 1 || x >= st.w - 1 || y <= 1 || y >= st.h - 1) return MAX_TILE_COUNT;
579	    const k = y * st.w + x;
580	    if (seen.has(k)) continue;
581	    seen.add(k);
582	    const i = st.idx(x, y);
583	    if (st.wall[i] === 244) return MAX_TILE_COUNT;
584	    if (st.liquid[i] > 0 && st.liquidType[i] === LIQUID_TYPE.SHIMMER) return MAX_TILE_COUNT;
585	    if (st.wall[i] !== 0) return MAX_TILE_COUNT;   // jungle=false 分支(cs:9531-9534)
586	    // lavaOk=true:岩浆只进 lavaCount,不截断
587	    if (!solidTileStrict(st, x, y)) {
588	      count++;
589	      stack.push([x - 1, y], [x + 1, y], [x, y - 1], [x, y + 1]);
590	    }
591	  }
592	  return count;
593	}
594	
595	export class DeadMansChestBiome {
596	  private readonly st: TileStore;
597	  private readonly rng: RNG;
598	  private readonly gs: GenState;
599	  private readonly darts: DartSpot[] = [];
600	  private readonly wires: WireSpot[] = [];
601	  private readonly boulders: BoulderSpot[] = [];
602	  private readonly explosives: Array<{ x: number; y: number }> = [];
603	
604	  constructor(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, gs: GenState) {
605	    this.st = st; this.rng = rng; this.gs = gs;
606	  }
607	
608	  /** GetPossibleChestsToTrapify(cs:405-442):全图金箱(style 1)扫描。
609	   *  原版按 Main.chest 下标序;此处栅格行扫描——候选集相同,每候选掷骰数一致(4),
610	   *  对后续全局 RNG 状态无影响。structures.CanPlace 检查无全局 StructureMap,跳过 */
611	  getPossibleChests(): Array<{ x: number; y: number }> {
612	    const st = this.st;
613	    const out: Array<{ x: number; y: number }> = [];
614	    for (let y = 0; y < st.h; y++) {
615	      for (let x = 0; x < st.w; x++) {
616	        const i = st.idx(x, y);
617	        // 金箱锚点:type 21 + style 1 + 2×2 左上角(frameX%36==0 且 /36==1 且 frameY==0)。
618	        // 原版经 Main.chest 下标天然只有锚点;栅格扫描须自剔除非锚点格(右格 frameX=54)
619	        if (st.type[i] !== CHEST || st.frameY[i] !== 0 || st.frameX[i] % 36 !== 0
620	          || Math.trunc(st.frameX[i] / 36) !== 1) continue;
621	        if (!this.isAGoodSpot(x, y)) continue;
622	        this.clearCaches();
623	        const px = x, py = y + 1;
624	        this.findBoulderTrapSpots(px, py);
625	        this.findDartTrapSpots(px, py);
626	        if (this.areThereEnoughTraps()) out.push({ x, y });
627	      }
628	    }
629	    return out;
630	  }
631	
632	  /** Place(cs:102-136) */
633	  place(ox: number, oy: number): boolean {
634	    if (!this.isAGoodSpot(ox, oy)) return false;
635	    this.clearCaches();
636	    const px = ox, py = oy + 1;   // position = origin + (0,1)
637	    this.findBoulderTrapSpots(px, py);
638	    this.findDartTrapSpots(px, py);
639	    this.findExplosiveTrapSpots(px, py);
640	    if (!this.areThereEnoughTraps()) return false;
641	    this.turnGoldChestIntoDeadMansChest(ox, oy);
642	    for (const d of this.darts) this.actuallyPlaceDartTrap(d);
643	    for (const w of this.wires) this.placeWireLine(w);
644	    for (const b of this.boulders) this.actuallyPlaceBoulderTrap(b);
645	    for (const e of this.explosives) this.actuallyPlaceExplosive(e);
646	    this.placeWiresForExplosives(ox, oy);
647	    return true;
648	  }
649	
650	  /** PlaceWiresForExplosives(cs:138-161) */
651	  private placeWiresForExplosives(ox: number, oy: number): void {
652	    if (this.explosives.length <= 0) return;
653	    const first = this.explosives[0];
654	    this.wires.push({ x: ox, y: oy, dirX: 0, dirY: 1, steps: first.y - oy });
655	    let minX = first.x, maxX = first.x;
656	    for (let i = 1; i < this.explosives.length; i++) {
657	      const x = this.explosives[i].x;
658	      if (minX > x) minX = x;
659	      if (maxX < x) maxX = x;
660	    }
661	    this.wires.push({ x: minX, y: first.y, dirX: 1, dirY: 0, steps: maxX - minX });
662	    for (const w of this.wires) this.placeWireLine(w);
663	  }
664	
665	  /** AreThereEnoughTraps(cs:163-170) */
666	  private areThereEnoughTraps(): boolean {
667	    if (this.boulders.length >= 1 || this.explosives.length >= 1) return this.darts.length >= 1;
668	    return false;
669	  }
670	
671	  private clearCaches(): void {
672	    this.darts.length = 0;
673	    this.wires.length = 0;
674	    this.boulders.length = 0;
675	    this.explosives.length = 0;
676	  }
677	
678	  /** FindBoulderTrapSpots(cs:180-220) */
679	  private findBoulderTrapSpots(px: number, py: number): void {
680	    const rng = this.rng;
681	    let x = px;
682	    const num = rng.int(2, 4);    // Next(_numberOfBoulderTraps = IntRange(2,4))
683	    const num2 = rng.int(2, 4);   // Next(_numberOfStepsBetweenBoulderTraps)
684	    x -= Math.trunc(num / 2) * num2;
685	    const num3 = py - 6;
686	    for (let i = 0; i <= num; i++) {
687	      this.findBoulderTrapSpot(x, num3);
688	      x += num2;
689	    }
690	    if (this.boulders.length <= 0) return;
691	    let minX = this.boulders[0].x, maxX = this.boulders[0].x;
692	    for (let j = 1; j < this.boulders.length; j++) {
693	      const bx = this.boulders[j].x;
694	      if (minX > bx) minX = bx;
695	      if (maxX < bx) maxX = bx;
696	    }
697	    if (minX > px) minX = px;
698	    if (maxX < px) maxX = px;
699	    this.wires.push({ x: minX, y: num3 - 1, dirX: 1, dirY: 0, steps: maxX - minX });
700	    this.wires.push({ x: px, y: py, dirX: 0, dirY: -1, steps: 7 });
701	  }
702	
703	  /** FindBoulderTrapSpot(cs:222-234):向上找首个实心 */
704	  private findBoulderTrapSpot(x: number, y: number): void {
705	    for (let i = 0; i < 50; i++) {
706	      if (activeAt(this.st, x, y - i)) {
707	        this.placeBoulderTrapSpot(x, y - i, i);
708	        break;
709	      }
710	    }
711	  }
712	
713	  /** PlaceBoulderTrapSpot(cs:236-290) */
714	  private placeBoulderTrapSpot(x: number, y: number, yPush: number): void {
715	    const st = this.st;
716	    const counts = new Map<number, number>();
717	    for (let i = x; i < x + 2; i++) {
718	      for (let j = y - 4; j <= y; j++) {
719	        if (!st.inBounds(i, j)) return;
720	        const idx = st.idx(i, j);
721	        const t = st.type[idx];
722	        if (st.flags[idx] && !TILE_DEFS[t]?.framed && !!TILE_DEFS[t]?.solid) {
723	          counts.set(t, (counts.get(t) ?? 0) + 1);
724	        }
725	        if ((st.flags[idx] && NOT_CLEARABLE.has(t)) || (st.flags[idx] && isContainer(t))) return;
726	      }
727	    }
728	    for (let k = x - 1; k < x + 3; k++) {
729	      for (let l = y - 5; l <= y - 2; l++) {
730	        if (!st.inBounds(k, l)) return;
731	        const idx = st.idx(k, l);
732	        if (!st.flags[idx] || isContainer(st.type[idx])) return;
733	      }
734	    }
735	    for (let m = x - 2; m <= x + 3; m++) {
736	      for (let n = y - 6; n <= y - 1; n++) {
737	        if (!st.inBounds(m, n)) continue;
738	        const idx = st.idx(m, n);
739	        if (st.flags[idx] && (isContainer(st.type[idx]) || BOULDER_FORBID.has(st.type[idx])
740	          || st.type[idx] === LIHARD || st.type[idx] === LIHARD_ALTAR)) return;
741	      }
742	    }
743	    let best = -1;
744	    // 原版按 type 升序扫全表取严格最大(cs:281-288)——平票取低 id,按 id 升序遍历对齐
745	    for (const t of [...counts.keys()].sort((a, b) => a - b)) {
746	      if (best === -1 || (counts.get(best) ?? 0) < (counts.get(t) ?? 0)) best = t;
747	    }
748	    // 原版全零计数时 num6 收敛到 0(泥土)——本仓 tile id 空间不同,取 DIRT 兜底
749	    this.boulders.push({ x, y, yPush: yPush - 1, bestType: best < 0 ? DIRT : best });
750	  }
751	
752	  /** FindDartTrapSpots(cs:292-308) */
753	  private findDartTrapSpots(px: number, py: number): void {
754	    const rng = this.rng;
755	    const num = rng.int(3, 6);    // Next(_numberOfDartTraps = IntRange(3,6))
756	    let dirX = rng.int(0, 1) !== 0 ? 1 : -1;   // Next(2) != 0 ? 1 : -1
757	    let steps = -1;
758	    let y = py;
759	    for (let i = 0; i < num; i++) {
760	      const found = this.findDartTrapSpotSingle(px, y, dirX);
761	      dirX *= -1;
762	      y--;
763	      if (found) steps = i;
764	    }
765	    this.wires.push({ x: px, y: y + num, dirX: 0, dirY: -1, steps });
766	  }
767	
768	  /** FindDartTrapSpotSingle(cs:310-328) */
769	  private findDartTrapSpotSingle(px: number, py: number, directionX: number): boolean {
770	    const st = this.st;
771	    for (let i = 0; i < 20; i++) {
772	      const x = px + i * directionX;
773	      if (!st.inBounds(x, py)) return false;
774	      const idx = st.idx(x, py);
775	      const t = st.type[idx];
776	      const active = st.flags[idx] !== 0;
777	      // 非容器 && 实心 → 5 格外且可清 → 飞镖锚点;否则失败
778	      if (!(active && isContainer(t)) && active && !!TILE_DEFS[t]?.solid) {
779	        if (i >= 5 && (st.wire[idx] & WIRE_ACTUATOR) === 0 && !TILE_DEFS[t]?.framed
780	          && !NOT_CLEARABLE.has(t)) {
781	          this.darts.push({ x: px, y: py, directionX, xPush: i, trapX: x, trapY: py });
782	          return true;
783	        }
784	        return false;
785	      }
786	    }
787	    return false;
788	  }
789	
790	  /** FindExplosiveTrapSpots(cs:330-389)。注意第三批候选不清空 list(原版如此,cs:364-376) */
791	  private findExplosiveTrapSpots(px: number, py: number): void {
792	    const rng = this.rng;
793	    let x = px;
794	    const y = py + 3;
795	    let list: number[] = [];
796	    if (this.isGoodSpotForExplosive(x, y)) list.push(x);
797	    x++;
798	    if (this.isGoodSpotForExplosive(x, y)) list.push(x);
799	    let num = -1;
800	    if (list.length > 0) num = list[rng.int(0, list.length - 1)];
801	    list = [];
802	    x += rng.int(2, 5);            // Next(2, 6)
803	    const span = 4;                // num2
804	    for (let i = x; i < x + span; i++) {
805	      if (this.isGoodSpotForExplosive(i, y)) list.push(i);
806	    }
807	    let num3 = -1;
808	    if (list.length > 0) num3 = list[rng.int(0, list.length - 1)];
809	    x = px - span - rng.int(2, 5);
810	    for (let j = x; j < x + span; j++) {
811	      if (this.isGoodSpotForExplosive(j, y)) list.push(j);   // ← 不清空(原版 cs:364-369)
812	    }
813	    let num4 = -1;
814	    if (list.length > 0) num4 = list[rng.int(0, list.length - 1)];
815	    if (num4 !== -1) this.explosives.push({ x: num4, y });
816	    if (num !== -1) this.explosives.push({ x: num, y });
817	    if (num3 !== -1) this.explosives.push({ x: num3, y });
818	  }
819	
820	  /** IsGoodSpotsForExplosive(cs:391-403):实心非框架非单向面地板 */
821	  private isGoodSpotForExplosive(x: number, y: number): boolean {
822	    const st = this.st;
823	    if (!st.inBounds(x, y)) return false;
824	    const idx = st.idx(x, y);
825	    const t = st.type[idx];
826	    if (st.flags[idx] && isContainer(t)) return false;
827	    if (st.flags[idx] && !!TILE_DEFS[t]?.solid && !TILE_DEFS[t]?.framed
828	      && !TILE_DEFS[t]?.platform) return true;
829	    return false;
830	  }
831	
832	  /** IsAGoodSpot(cs:444-477) */
833	  private isAGoodSpot(x: number, y: number): boolean {
834	    const st = this.st;
835	    if (!(x >= 50 && y >= 50 && x < st.w - 50 && y < st.h - 50)) return false;  // InWorld(50)
836	    // oceanDepths(cs:10978):y ≤ oceanLevel 且海滩带
837	    const oceanLevel = (this.gs.worldSurface + this.gs.rockLevel) / 2 + 40;
838	    if (y <= oceanLevel && (x < this.gs.beachDistance || x > st.w - this.gs.beachDistance)) return false;
839	    const idx = st.idx(x, y);
840	    if (st.type[idx] !== CHEST) return false;
841	    if (Math.trunc(st.frameX[idx] / 36) !== 1) return false;
842	    const below = st.idx(x, y + 2);
843	    if (NOT_CLEARABLE.has(st.type[below])) return false;   // CanBeClearedDuringGeneration
844	    if (countWires(st, x, y, 20) > 0) return false;
845	    if (countTiles(st, x, y) < 40) return false;
846	    return true;
847	  }
848	
849	  /** TurnGoldChestIntoDeadMansChest(cs:479-514) */
850	  private turnGoldChestIntoDeadMansChest(x: number, y: number): void {
851	    const st = this.st;
852	    for (let i = 0; i < 2; i++) {
853	      for (let j = 0; j < 2; j++) {
854	        const idx = st.idx(x + i, y + j);
855	        st.type[idx] = CHEST2;
856	        st.frameX[idx] = 144 + i * 18;
857	        st.frameY[idx] = j * 18;
858	      }
859	    }
860	    // 1/3 战利品前插(Item 5007):掷骰保留(1 次 Next(3));本 pass 无 chest sink,写入跳过
861	    this.rng.int(0, 2);
862	  }
863	
864	  /** ActuallyPlaceDartTrap(cs:516-532) */
865	  private actuallyPlaceDartTrap(d: DartSpot): void {
866	    const st = this.st;
867	    const idx = st.idx(d.trapX, d.trapY);
868	    st.type[idx] = DART_TRAP;
869	    st.flags[idx] = 1;
870	    st.frameX[idx] = d.directionX === -1 ? 18 : 0;
871	    st.frameY[idx] = 0;
872	    st.slope[idx] = 0;
873	    st.half[idx] = 0;
874	    this.placeWireLine({ x: d.x, y: d.y, dirX: d.directionX, dirY: 0, steps: d.xPush });
875	  }
876	
877	  /** PlaceWireLine(cs:534-540):0..steps 含端点 */
878	  private placeWireLine(w: WireSpot): void {
879	    const st = this.st;
880	    for (let i = 0; i <= w.steps; i++) {
881	      const x = w.x + w.dirX * i, y = w.y + w.dirY * i;
882	      if (st.inBounds(x, y)) st.wire[st.idx(x, y)] |= WIRE_RED;
883	    }
884	  }
885	
886	  /** ActuallyPlaceBoulderTrap(cs:542-609) */
887	  private actuallyPlaceBoulderTrap(b: BoulderSpot): void {
888	    const st = this.st;
889	    const requiredHeight = 4;   // requiredHeight 恒 4(cs:289)
890	    for (let i = b.x; i < b.x + 2; i++) {
891	      for (let j = b.y - requiredHeight; j <= b.y + 2; j++) {
892	        if (!st.inBounds(i, j)) continue;
893	        const idx = st.idx(i, j);
894	        if (j < b.y - requiredHeight + 2) {
895	          st.setTileSilent(i, j, 0);                       // ClearTile
896	        } else if (j <= b.y) {
897	          if (!st.flags[idx]) {
898	            st.type[idx] = b.bestType;
899	            st.flags[idx] = 1;
900	          }
901	          st.slope[idx] = 0;
902	          st.half[idx] = 0;
903	          st.wire[idx] |= WIRE_RED;
904	          if (TILE_DEFS[st.type[idx]]?.solid) st.wire[idx] |= WIRE_ACTUATOR;
905	        } else {
906	          st.setTileSilent(i, j, 0);                       // ClearTile
907	        }
908	      }
909	    }
910	    // 6×6 石壳(cs:585-606):非巨石格 → 石头;带线格补致动器
911	    const cx = b.x + 1, cy = b.y - requiredHeight + 1;
912	    for (let k = cx - 3; k <= cx + 2; k++) {
913	      for (let l = cy - 3; l <= cy + 2; l++) {
914	        if (!st.inBounds(k, l)) continue;
915	        const idx = st.idx(k, l);
916	        if (!st.flags[idx]) continue;   // 近似:仅活性格参与(原版对惰性格也写 type)
917	        if (!BOULDER_TILES.has(st.type[idx])) {
918	          st.type[idx] = STONE;
919	          if (st.wire[idx] & 15) st.wire[idx] |= WIRE_ACTUATOR;
920	        }
921	      }
922	    }
923	    // PlaceTile(cx, cy, 138):巨石(cs:607)——TileObjectData Style2x2 四格分片帧
924	    // (Tiles_138 36×38 单变体;此前只写锚定格 = 渲染只剩 18×18 小片,同 TrapsPass
925	    // "贴图不全"根因,2026-08-13)
926	    if (st.inBounds(cx, cy) && !st.flags[st.idx(cx, cy)]) {
927	      for (let dy = 0; dy < 2; dy++) {
928	        for (let dx = 0; dx < 2; dx++) {
929	          if (st.inBounds(cx + dx, cy + dy)) st.setTileSilent(cx + dx, cy + dy, BOULDER, dx * 18, dy * 18);
930	        }
931	      }
932	    }
933	    this.placeWireLine({ x: b.x, y: b.y, dirX: 0, dirY: 1, steps: b.yPush });
934	  }
935	
936	  /** ActuallyPlaceExplosive(cs:611-619) */
937	  private actuallyPlaceExplosive(e: { x: number; y: number }): void {
938	    const st = this.st;
939	    if (!st.inBounds(e.x, e.y)) return;
940	    const idx = st.idx(e.x, e.y);
941	    st.type[idx] = EXPLOSIVES;
942	    st.flags[idx] = 1;
943	    st.frameX[idx] = 0;
944	    st.frameY[idx] = 0;
945	    st.slope[idx] = 0;
946	    st.half[idx] = 0;
947	  }
948	}
949	
950	// ============================================================
951	// 2. ThinIceBiome(Terraria.GameContent.Biomes/ThinIceBiome.cs 全文)
952	// ============================================================
953	
954	/** ThinIceBiome.Place(cs:9-34):雪原水面上 BreakableIce 162 斑 */
955	export function placeThinIcePatch(
956	  st: TileStore, rng: RNG, protectedRects: Rect[], ox: number, oy: number,
957	): boolean {
958	  // TileScanner 50×50(cs:11-12,origin-25 起):0/1/59/147/225 计数
959	  let dirtStone = 0, mud = 0, snow = 0, hive = 0;
960	  for (let dx = 0; dx < 50; dx++) {
961	    for (let dy = 0; dy < 50; dy++) {
962	      const x = ox - 25 + dx, y = oy - 25 + dy;
963	      if (!st.inBounds(x, y)) continue;
964	      const idx = st.idx(x, y);
965	      if (!st.flags[idx]) continue;
966	      const t = st.type[idx];
967	      if (t === DIRT || t === STONE) dirtStone++;
968	      else if (t === MUD) mud++;
969	      else if (t === SNOW_BLOCK) snow++;
970	      else if (t === HIVE) hive++;
971	    }
972	  }
973	  if (hive > 0) return false;                    // cs:16-18
974	  if (snow <= mud || snow <= dirtStone) return false;   // cs:19-22
975	  let num4 = 0;
976	  // for (num5 = Next(10,15); num5 > 5; num5--)(cs:24-31)
977	  for (let num5 = rng.int(10, 14); num5 > 5; num5--) {
978	    const num6 = rng.int(-5, 4);                 // Next(-5, 5)
979	    const cx = ox + num6, cy = oy + num4;
980	    // Gen 1:Circle(num5) + Blotches(4) + OnlyTiles(147,161,224,0,1) → SetTile(162)
981	    forEachCircleCell(st, cx, cy, num5, rng, 4, (x, y) => {
982	      const idx = st.idx(x, y);
983	      if (!st.flags[idx] || !ICE_SRC.has(st.type[idx])) return;
984	      setThinIce(st, idx);
985	    });
986	    // Gen 2:Circle(num5) + Blotches(4) + HasLiquid + SkipTiles(21,467,226,237)
987	    //        → SetTile(162) + SetLiquid(0,0)
988	    forEachCircleCell(st, cx, cy, num5, rng, 4, (x, y) => {
989	      const idx = st.idx(x, y);
990	      if (st.liquid[idx] === 0) return;          // HasLiquid
991	      if (st.flags[idx] && ICE_SKIP.has(st.type[idx])) return;   // SkipTiles
992	      setThinIce(st, idx);
993	      st.liquid[idx] = 0;                        // SetLiquid(0, 0)
994	      st.liquidType[idx] = 0;
995	    });
996	    num4 += num5 - 2;
997	  }
998	  // structures.AddStructure((ox-25, oy-25, 50, 50), 8)(cs:32)
999	  protectedRects.push({ x0: ox - 25, y0: oy - 25, x1: ox + 25, y1: oy + 25 });
1000	  return true;
1001	}
1002	
1003	/** Shapes.Circle(Shapes.cs:33-49) 行主序 + Modifiers.Blotches(Modifiers.cs:89-137)。
1004	 *  Blotches(scale):先掷 1 次 NextDouble 丢弃,再判 chance=0.3;< 0.3 时掷 4 次
1005	 *  Next 得 blotch 矩形(x∈[-3,0]..[0,3] × y 同),否则单格 */
1006	function forEachCircleCell(
1007	  st: TileStore, cx: number, cy: number, radius: number, rng: RNG, _scale: number,
1008	  apply: (x: number, y: number) => void,
1009	): void {
1010	  void _scale;   // Blotches 尺度恒 4(ThinIceBiome 固定传参)
1011	  const rr = (radius + 1) * (radius + 1);
1012	  for (let dy = -radius; dy <= radius; dy++) {
1013	    const num2 = (radius / radius) * dy;   // horizontalRadius == verticalRadius
1014	    const half = Math.min(radius, Math.trunc(Math.sqrt(rr - num2 * num2)));
1015	    for (let dx = -half; dx <= half; dx++) {
1016	      rng.next();                          // Blotches.Apply 丢弃掷
1017	      if (rng.next() < 0.3) {
1018	        const bx0 = rng.int(-3, 0), bx1 = rng.int(0, 3);
1019	        const by0 = rng.int(-3, 0), by1 = rng.int(0, 3);
1020	        for (let bi = bx0; bi <= bx1; bi++) {
1021	          for (let bj = by0; bj <= by1; bj++) {
1022	            const x = cx + dx + bi, y = cy + dy + bj;
1023	            if (st.inBounds(x, y)) apply(x, y);
1024	          }
1025	        }
1026	      } else {
1027	        const x = cx + dx, y = cy + dy;
1028	        if (st.inBounds(x, y)) apply(x, y);
1029	      }
1030	    }
1031	  }
1032	}
1033	
1034	/** Actions.SetTile(162, setSelfFrames)(Actions.cs:203-227):
1035	 *  Clear(~(Wiring|Actuator)) = 清 tile/wall/liquid/帧/坡/半砖,保留线与致动位;
1036	 *  生成期 TileFrame 对非 frameImportant 格无操作 → 帧落 0 */
1037	function setThinIce(st: TileStore, idx: number): void {
1038	  st.type[idx] = THIN_ICE;
1039	  st.flags[idx] = 1;
1040	  st.frameX[idx] = 0;
1041	  st.frameY[idx] = 0;
1042	  st.wall[idx] = 0;
1043	  st.liquid[idx] = 0;
1044	  st.liquidType[idx] = 0;
1045	  st.half[idx] = 0;
1046	  st.slope[idx] = 0;
1047	}
1048	
1049	// ============================================================
1050	// 3. placeLavaTrap(WorldGen.cs:8648-8732;调用点 cs:22093-22127)
1051	// ============================================================
1052	
1053	/** 地狱熔岩陷阱:岩浆湖底掏腔,湖底层致动石栅 + 压板 135 style7 + 红线。
1054	 *  门禁:锚点须满格岩浆;11×11 内 ≥50 满岩浆格;±20 无线;腔高 ≤40 且 ≥4 */
1055	export function placeLavaTrap(st: TileStore, gs: GenState, x: number, y: number): boolean {
1056	  void gs;
1057	  if (!st.inBounds(x, y)) return false;
1058	  const num = 5, minLava = 50, maxDrop = 40, scanR = 20, minGap = 4;
1059	  const i0 = st.idx(x, y);
1060	  if (st.flags[i0] || st.liquid[i0] !== 255 || st.liquidType[i0] !== LIQUID_TYPE.LAVA) return false;
1061	  let lavaCells = 0;
1062	  for (let dx = -num; dx <= num; dx++) {
1063	    for (let dy = -num; dy <= num; dy++) {
1064	      if (!st.inBounds(x + dx, y + dy)) continue;
1065	      const idx = st.idx(x + dx, y + dy);
1066	      if (!st.flags[idx] && st.liquid[idx] === 255 && st.liquidType[idx] === LIQUID_TYPE.LAVA) lavaCells++;
1067	    }
1068	  }
1069	  if (lavaCells < minLava) return false;
1070	  // 下沉到岩浆湖底首个实心(cs:8666-8674)
1071	  let num7 = y;
1072	  for (;;) {

1074	    num7++;
1075	    if (num7 > st.h - 200 || num7 >= st.h) return false;
1076	  }
1077	  if (!solidAt(st, x, num7) || solidTopAt(st, x, num7)) return false;
1078	  const num8 = num7;
1079	  // ±20 × ±20 无任何线(cs:8681-8689)
1080	  for (let k = x - scanR; k <= x + scanR; k++) {

1084	  }
1085	  // 穿透湖底实心层(cs:8690-8696)
1086	  while (activeAt(st, x, num7) && solidAt(st, x, num7) && !solidTopAt(st, x, num7)) {
1087	    num7++;
1088	    if (num7 > st.h - 200 || num7 >= st.h) return false;
1089	  }
1090	  const belowIdx = st.idx(x, num7 - 1);
1091	  if (DUNGEON_TILES.has(st.type[belowIdx]) || st.type[belowIdx] === HIVE || st.type[belowIdx] === LIHARD) {
1092	    return false;
1093	  }
1094	  // 腔顶下行到腔底实心(cs:8705-8714)
1095	  const num9 = num7;
1096	  for (;;) {
1097	    if (activeAt(st, x, num7)) break;
1098	    num7++;
1099	    if (num7 > st.h - 200 || num7 >= st.h) return false;
1100	    if (st.liquid[st.idx(x, num7)] > 0) return false;
1101	  }
1102	  const floorIdx = st.idx(x, num7);
1103	  if (!solidAt(st, x, num7) || solidTopAt(st, x, num7) || st.type[floorIdx] === THIN_ICE) return false;
1104	  const num10 = num7 - 1;
1105	  if (num10 - num8 > maxDrop) return false;
1106	  if (num10 - num9 < minGap) return false;
1107	  st.slope[floorIdx] = 0;
1108	  st.half[floorIdx] = 0;
1109	  // PlaceTile(x, num10, 135, style 7)(cs:8720)——style 在 frameY(126)
1110	  st.setTileSilent(x, num10, PLATE, 0, 126);
1111	  // 线 + 致动石栅(cs:8721-8730):num8..num9-1 致动,num8..num10 红线
1112	  for (let m = num8; m <= num10; m++) {
1113	    if (!st.inBounds(x, m)) continue;
1114	    const idx = st.idx(x, m);
1115	    st.wire[idx] |= WIRE_RED;
1116	    if (m < num9) {
1117	      st.slope[idx] = 0;
1118	      st.half[idx] = 0;
1119	      st.wire[idx] |= WIRE_ACTUATOR;
1120	    }
1121	  }
1122	  return true;
1123	}
1124

218	      if (flags.ivy) {
219	        if (!gs.gennedMahoganyWands || rng.int(0, 4) === 0) {
220	          gs.gennedMahoganyWands = true;
221	          push(3360); push(3361);
222	        }
223	        if (rng.int(0, 9) === 0) push(4426);
224	        if (rng.int(0, 9) === 0) push(5525);
225	      }
226	      if (flags.water) {
227	        if (rng.int(0, 1) === 0) push(4425);
228	        if (rng.int(0, 1) === 0) push(4460);
229	      }
230	      // 蘑菇箱 flag7（cs:37007-37021：1/2 矿车 否则三件套）
231	      if (flags.mushroom) {
232	        if (rng.int(0, 1) === 0) push(4450);
233	        else { push(4779); push(4780); push(4781); }
234	      }
235	      // 地牢箱 flag9 门（cs:36892-36906，flag10 上锁箱不给钥匙）
236	      if (flags.dungeon && !flags.lockedBiome) {
237	        if (!gs.generatedShadowKey || rng.int(0, 2) === 0) {
238	          gs.generatedShadowKey = true;
239	          push(329);
240	        }
241	        if (!gs.generatedRamRune || rng.int(0, 7) === 0) {
242	          gs.generatedRamRune = true;
243	          push(5465);
244	        }
245	      }
246	    } else {
247	      // 主件(cs:36911-36975)：num25=Next(7) 先掷恒消耗（种子对齐），再掷 906/997 门
248	      const num25 = rng.int(0, 6);
249	      if (rng.int(0, 19) === 0 && gy > gs.lavaLine) push(906);
250	      else if (rng.int(0, 14) === 0) push(997);
251	      else {
252	        if (num25 === 6) { push(930); push(931, rng.int(25, 50)); }
253	        else push([49, 50, 53, 54, 5011, 975][num25]);
254	      }
255	    }
256	    if (rng.int(0, 4) === 0) push(flags.ice ? 5120 : 43);     // 1/5(冰箱特殊项/可疑眼球)
257	    if (rng.int(0, 2) === 0) push(167);                       // 1/3 炸药
258	    if (rng.int(0, 3) === 0) push(51, rng.int(25, 50));       // 1/4 小丑箭
259	    if (rng.int(0, 1) === 0) push(rng.int(0, 1) === 0 ? bars.gold : bars.silver, rng.int(3, 10));
260	    if (rng.int(0, 1) === 0) push(rng.int(0, 1) === 0 ? 41 : 279, rng.int(25, 50)); // 烈焰箭/飞刀
261	    if (rng.int(0, 1) === 0) push(188, rng.int(3, 5));        // 治疗药水
262	    if (rng.int(0, 2) > 0) push(pick([296, 295, 299, 302, 303, 305]), rng.int(1, 2));
263	    if (rng.int(0, 2) > 1) push(pick([301, 297, 304, 2329, 2351, 2326]), rng.int(1, 2));
264	    if (rng.int(0, 1) === 0) push(2350, rng.int(2, 4));
265	    if (rng.int(0, 1) === 0) push(rng.int(0, 1) === 0 ? (flags.ice ? 974 : 8) : 282, rng.int(15, 30)); // 火把/荧光棒
266	    if (rng.int(0, 1) === 0) push(73, rng.int(1, 2));         // 金币
267	  } else {
268	    // ===== 地狱箱(gy > h-205,cs:37163-37360;与 style 4 同门)=====
269	    if (mainVanillaId > 0) {
270	      push(mainVanillaId);
271	      if (rng.int(0, 4) === 0) push(5010);                    // 1/5 宝藏磁铁
272	      if (rng.int(0, 9) === 0) push(4443);
273	      if (rng.int(0, 9) === 0) push(4737);
274	      if (rng.int(0, 9) === 0) push(4551);
275	    } else {
276	      push([49, 50, 53, 54][rng.int(0, 3)]);
277	    }
278	    if (rng.int(0, 2) === 0) push(167);                       // 1/3 炸药
279	    if (rng.int(0, 1) === 0) push(rng.int(0, 1) === 0 ? 117 : bars.gold, rng.int(15, 29)); // 陨铁锭/金锭
280	    if (rng.int(0, 1) === 0) {
281	      // 地狱火箭矢 或 银弹(银矿为钨时换钨弹 4915,cs:37242)
282	      const silverIsTungsten = (TILE_DEFS[gs.oreTiers.silver]?.key ?? '').includes('tungsten');
283	      push(rng.int(0, 1) === 0 ? 265 : (silverIsTungsten ? 4915 : 278), rng.int(50, 74));
284	    }
285	    if (rng.int(0, 1) === 0) push(227, rng.int(15, 20));      // 再生药水 cs:37290 Next(6)+15=[15,20]（曾 15,29）
286	    if (rng.int(0, 3) > 0) push(pick([296, 295, 293, 288, 294, 297, 304, 2323]), rng.int(1, 2));
287	    if (rng.int(0, 2) > 1) push(pick([305, 301, 302, 288, 300, 2351, 2348, 2345]), rng.int(1, 2));
288	    { // cs:37375-37376：先掷 stack27=Next(1,3) 再 Next(2) 选物（顺序对齐种子流）
289	      const stack27 = rng.int(1, 2);
290	      push(rng.int(0, 1) === 0 ? 2350 : 4870, stack27); // 回城/回归药水
291	    }
292	    if (rng.int(0, 1) === 0) push(rng.int(0, 1) === 0 ? 8 : 282, rng.int(15, 29));    // 火把/荧光棒
293	    if (rng.int(0, 1) === 0) push(73, rng.int(2, 4));         // 金币
294	  }
295	
296	  // ---- 尾段(theme 附加,cs:37361-37560;tile 21 箱)----
297	  const items: Array<{ id: number; stack: number } | null> = out.map((it) => ({ ...it }));
298	  // 主题尾件
299	  if (flags.ivy && rng.int(0, 3) === 0) {
300	    const id = vid(2204);
301	    if (id >= 0 && items.length < 40) items.push({ id, stack: 1 });
302	  }
303	  if (flags.ice && rng.int(0, 6) === 0) {
304	    const id = vid(2198);
305	    if (id >= 0 && items.length < 40) items.push({ id, stack: 1 });
306	  }
307	  // flag12 天空主题（cs:37424-37474）：1/3 天空磨坊；Next(6) 五选一装饰画；
308	  // 751 云 [50,100]（cs:37469）
309	  {
310	    const f12 = !!flags.skyTheme || mainVanillaId === 159 || mainVanillaId === 65
311	      || mainVanillaId === 158 || mainVanillaId === 2219;
312	    if (f12 && rng.int(0, 2) === 0) push(2197);
313	    if (flags.temple) push(2195); // flag13（cs:37428）：蜥蜴炉恒给
314	    if (flags.dungeon && rng.int(0, 7) === 0) push(2192); // flag9（cs:37432）1/8 血腥砍刀
315	    if (f12) {
316	      const n38 = rng.int(0, 5);
317	      push([5258, 5226, 5254, 5238, 5255, 5388][n38]); // cs:37440-37468
318	      push(751, rng.int(50, 100));
319	    }
320	    // 上锁生物群系箱 1/2 → 5234 虔诚残片（cs:37494-37498）
321	    if (flags.lockedBiome && rng.int(0, 1) === 0) push(5234);
322	    if (flags.temple) { // flag13 尾（cs:37479-37491）：1/5 日耀碑 否则碎片 [3,7]
323	      if (rng.int(0, 4) === 0) push(2767);
324	      else push(2766, rng.int(3, 7));
325	    }
326	    // voice 门（cs:37557：恒消耗 1 掷）——1/12 命中由调用方后处理（此处保流对齐）
327	    rng.int(0, 11);
328	  }
329	  // 键主件(如金字塔/蛛网/世界树的特殊掉落直接给 key)置首。
330	  // 同一原版物品可能有两个注册键:调用方显式键(如 vi_832_living_wood_wand)与
331	  // vid() 的规范驼峰键(items.ts 自动注册循环 vi_832_LivingWoodWand)。mainKey 与
332	  // mainVanillaId 同源(placeBuriedChest 由键反解)时,先剔除 push(mainVanillaId)
333	  // 经 vid() 入箱的那份,再按调用方指定键入箱——否则世界树宝箱会出现两根活木魔棒。
334	  if (ctx.mainKey) {
335	    const keyId = ITEM_BY_KEY[ctx.mainKey];
336	    if (keyId !== undefined) {
337	      if (vanillaIdOfKey(ctx.mainKey) !== null && vanillaIdOfKey(ctx.mainKey) === mainVanillaId) {
338	        const viaVid = vid(mainVanillaId);
339	        const idx = items.findIndex((it) => it && it.id === viaVid);
340	        if (idx >= 0) items.splice(idx, 1);
341	      }
342	      if (!items.some((it) => it && it.id === keyId)) items.unshift({ id: keyId, stack: 1 });
343	    }
344	  }
345	  return items;
346	}

348	/** AddBuriedChest（1456 cs:35920）下落/门禁段 1:1：
349	 *  自 j 向下扫至 k < maxTilesY-10，逐行检查 anyShimmer / 幼虫 231 → 整体失败；
350	 *  首个 SolidTile（半砖/坡面不算，WorldGen.SolidTile）落定。
351	 *  ±2 邻域（InWorld 100 边距）有 Boulders{138,484,664,665,711-716}/祭坛 26/蜥蜴祭坛 237 → 失败。
352	 *  样式/战利品解析与 2×2 放置沿用本仓库 rollChestLoot 管线。 */
353	export function placeBuriedChest(
354	  st: TileStore, rng: RNG, gs: GenState, chests: ChestSink,
355	  i: number, j: number, opts: { style?: number; tile?: number; loot?: number | string; flags?: ChestFlags; trySlope?: boolean } = {},
356	): boolean {
357	  // ±2 内不得有滚石/祭坛（cs:35957-35966：InWorld(n,num6,100) && Boulders || 26 || 237）
358	  for (let dx = -2; dx <= 2; dx++) {
359	    for (let dy = -2; dy <= 2; dy++) {
360	      const xx = i + dx, yy = j + dy;
361	      if (xx < 100 || yy < 100 || xx >= st.w - 100 || yy >= st.h - 100) continue;
362	      const k = st.idx(xx, yy);
363	      if (!st.flags[k]) continue;
364	      const t = st.type[k];
365	      if (BOULDER_TILES.has(t) || t === ALTAR || t === TALTAR) return false;
366	    }
367	  }
368	  // 下落到地面（cs:35931 `for k=j; k<maxTilesY-10; k++`）
369	  let gy = j;
370	  let slopeL = -1, slopeR = -1;   // trySlope 压平的坡度（失败时回填，cs:37567-37575）
371	  for (; gy < st.h - 10; gy++) {
372	    const k = st.idx(i, gy);
373	    if (st.liquid[k] > 0 && st.liquidType[k] === 4) return false;              // anyShimmer → 整体失败
374	    if (st.flags[k] && st.type[k] === LARVA) return false;                      // 幼虫 231 → 整体失败
375	    if (solidTileHB(st, i, gy)) {
376	      // trySlope 门（cs:35951-35968）：style 17 时 ±30 盒内不得有箱/越界；
377	      // 压平 (i-1,gy)/(i,gy) 的顶坡供放置（失败时回填）
378	      if (opts.trySlope && opts.style === 17) {
379	        for (let l = i - 30; l <= i + 30; l++) {
380	          for (let m = gy - 30; m <= gy + 30; m++) {
381	            if (l < 5 || m < 5 || l >= st.w - 5 || m >= st.h - 5) return false;  // InWorld(l,m,5)
382	            const nk = st.idx(l, m);
383	            if (st.flags[nk] && (st.type[nk] === CHEST || st.type[nk] === CHEST2)) return false;
384	          }
385	        }
386	      }
387	      if (opts.trySlope) {
388	        if (st.slope[st.idx(i - 1, gy)] >= 1 && st.slope[st.idx(i - 1, gy)] <= 2) {
389	          slopeL = st.slope[st.idx(i - 1, gy)];
390	          st.slope[st.idx(i - 1, gy)] = 0;
391	        }
392	        if (st.slope[k] >= 1 && st.slope[k] <= 2) {
393	          slopeR = st.slope[k];
394	          st.slope[k] = 0;
395	        }
396	      }
397	      break;
398	    }
399	  }
400	  if (gy >= st.h - 10) return false;
401	  if (!solidTileHB(st, i, gy)) return false;
402	  // trySlope 失败回填（cs:37567-37575：仅压平过的格才恢复）
403	  const restoreSlopes = (): void => {
404	    if (slopeL > -1) st.slope[st.idx(i - 1, gy)] = slopeL;
405	    if (slopeR > -1) st.slope[st.idx(i, gy)] = slopeR;
406	  };
407	
408	  let tile = opts.tile ?? CHEST;
409	  let style = opts.style;
410	  const flags: ChestFlags = { ...(opts.flags ?? {}) };
411	  let mainId = 0;
412	  let mainKey: string | null = null;
413	  if (typeof opts.loot === 'string') {
414	    mainKey = opts.loot;
415	    mainId = vanillaIdOfKey(opts.loot) ?? 0;
416	    if (mainId === 0 && ITEM_BY_KEY[opts.loot] === undefined) mainKey = null; // 无效键丢弃
417	  } else if (opts.loot !== undefined) {
418	    mainId = opts.loot;
419	  }
420	  if (style === undefined) {
421	    if (gy > st.h - 205) {
422	      // 地狱箱:style 4 + 地狱武器序(cs:36158-36171)
423	      style = 4;
424	      flags.hell = true;
425	      if (!mainId) mainId = nextHellItem(gs);
426	    } else {
427	      style = gy >= gs.worldSurface + 25 ? 1 : 0;
428	      if (style === 0) flags.wooden = true;
429	    }
430	  }
431	  // ---- 冰箱门（cs:36107-36130 1:1）：三析取——(tile21&&style11) ||
432	  // (tile467&&style24) || (无主件 && 位置 && 脚下冰系)。★原版显式传 style 11
433	  // 也进本池（HouseBuilder 冰屋正是此路径）——旧实现 style===undefined 单门漏此
434	  const tBelow = st.type[st.idx(i, gy)];
435	  if ((tile === CHEST && style === 11) || (tile === CHEST2 && style === 24)
436	    || (!mainId && gy >= gs.worldSurface + 25 && gy <= st.h - 205
437	      && (tBelow === SNOW || tBelow === ICE || tBelow === THIN_ICE))) {
438	    flags.ice = true;
439	    if (!mainId) {
440	      mainId = [670, 724, 950, 1319, 987, 1579][rng.int(0, 5)]; // cs:36121-36125
441	      if (rng.int(0, 19) === 0) mainId = 997; // 1/20 提炼机
442	      if (rng.int(0, 49) === 0) mainId = 669; // 1/50 鱼
443	    }
444	  }
445	  // ---- 沙漠箱门（cs:36066-36091 1:1）：三析取——(tile467&&style10) ||
446	  // (tile21&&style42&&flag16) || (无主件 && ≤h-205 && IsUndergroundDesert)
447	  const ud = gs.undergroundDesert;
448	  const inUd = !mainId && ud && i >= ud.x0 && i <= ud.x1 && gy >= ud.y0 && gy <= ud.y1 && gy <= st.h - 205;
449	  if ((tile === CHEST2 && style === 10) || inUd) {
450	    tile = CHEST2;
451	    style = 10;
452	    flags.desert = true;
453	    // cs:36084：分位点用蜂巢**实际雕刻范围**（desertHiveHigh/Low，PlaceClustersArea
454	    // 逐点收缩），非静态矩形 ±10（曾用 ud.y0/y1 → 分层界偏移）
455	    const deep = gy > (gs.desertHiveHigh * 3 + gs.desertHiveLow * 4) / 7;
456	    if (!mainId) {
457	      mainId = deep
458	        ? [4061, 4062, 4276][rng.int(0, 2)]
459	        : [4056, 4055, 4262, 4263][rng.int(0, 3)];
460	    }
461	  }
462	
463	  // 2×2 放置:顶行 frameY=0、frameX = style*36 + dx*18(PlaceChestDirect 语义)
464	  const topY = gy - 2;
465	  if (topY < 1) { restoreSlopes(); return false; }
466	  for (let dx = 0; dx < 2; dx++) {
467	    for (let dy = 0; dy < 2; dy++) {
468	      if (!st.inBounds(i + dx, topY + dy) || st.flags[st.idx(i + dx, topY + dy)]) { restoreSlopes(); return false; }
469	    }
470	  }
471	  // 样式派生 flag 族（cs:36201-36229）：32 蘑菇 / 16 神庙 / 13 天空主题
472	  if (style === 32) flags.mushroom = true;
473	  if (style === 16) flags.temple = true;
474	  if (style === 13) flags.skyTheme = true;
475	  if (!st.inBounds(i + 1, gy) || !st.flags[st.idx(i + 1, gy)]
476	    || !TILE_DEFS[st.type[st.idx(i + 1, gy)]]?.solid) { restoreSlopes(); return false; }
477	  const fw = TILE_DEFS[tile].vanilla?.fw ?? 2;
478	  for (let dx = 0; dx < 2; dx++) {
479	    for (let dy = 0; dy < 2; dy++) {
480	      st.setTileSilent(i + dx, topY + dy, tile, style * fw * 18 + dx * 18, dy * 18);
481	    }
482	  }
483	  // 战利品:四深度分支完整表(此前仅主件+火把/药水,体感稀缺主因之一)
484	  const items = rollChestLoot({ rng, gs, gy, flags, mainKey, worldH: st.h }, mainId);
485	  chests.push({ x: i, y: topY, items });
486	  if (flags.hell) gs.hellChestIdx++; // cs:36257-36264 放置成功才前进（失败重试取同一件）
487	  return true;
488	}
489	
490	/** Buried Chests pass:L7907 洞穴段(CaveChestCount 35-40 × WorldArea/5040000)。
491	 *  ★地狱段拆出 runUnderworldChestsPass:本函数曾在 hellPass 之前执行,
492	 *  地狱箱被后续地狱地形重写 100% 抹除(实际 0 个) */
493	export function runBuriedChestsPass(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, gs: GenState, chests: ChestSink): void {
494	  const { w, h } = st;
495	  const yMin = Math.floor((gs.worldSurfaceHigh + 20 + gs.rockLevel) / 2);
496	  // 洞穴箱:35-40 × 面积缩放(Configuration.json CaveChestCount ScaleWith:WorldArea;
497	  // 小世界(4200×1200=5040000)= 1.0 → 35-40;中 ×2.29;大 ×4.0)
498	  const target = Math.round(rng.int(35, 40) * (w * h) / 5040000);
499	  let fail = 10000;
500	  for (let n = 0; n < target && fail > 0; ) {
501	    const x = rng.int(20, w - 21);
502	    const y = rng.int(yMin, h - 231);
503	    const wl = st.inBounds(x, y) ? st.wall[st.idx(x, y)] : -1;
504	    // cs:17109：wallDungeon[wall] || wall == 87（神庙墙 87 独立门）→ 整体重掷
505	    if (isDungeonWall(wl) || wl === 87 || !placeBuriedChest(st, rng, gs, chests, x, y)) {
506	      fail--; continue;
507	    }
508	    n++;
509	  }
510	}
511	
512	/** 地狱箱(UnderworldChestCount 10-15 × WorldWidth/4200)。
513	 *  必须在 hellPass + 地狱屋之后调用(原版 Underworld(cs:13697) < Buried Chests(cs:17066)) */
514	export function runUnderworldChestsPass(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, gs: GenState, chests: ChestSink): void {
515	  const { w, h } = st;
516	  const target = Math.round(rng.int(10, 15) * (w / 4200));
517	  let fail = 10000;
518	  for (let n = 0; n < target && fail > 0; ) {
519	    const x = rng.int(20, w - 21);
520	    const y = rng.int(h - 200, h - 51);
521	    if (!placeBuriedChest(st, rng, gs, chests, x, y)) {
522	      fail--; continue;
523	    }
524	    n++;
525	  }
526	}
527	
528	/** Surface Chests pass(L8019):w*0.005 个地表木箱(style 0;墙 244 → 天空箱 style 12) */
529	export function runSurfaceChestsPass(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, gs: GenState, chests: ChestSink): void {
530	  const { w } = st;
531	  const count = Math.floor(w * 0.005);
532	  for (let n = 0; n < count; n++) {
533	    let ok = false;
534	    for (let tries = 0; tries < 2000 && !ok; tries++) {
535	      // cs:17244 首掷 Next(200, w-200);仅落点命中 oceanDepths(cs:17246-17254:
536	      // y ≤ oceanLevel=(ws+rl)/2+40 且 x 在海滩带内)才改掷 Next(300, w-300)——
537	      // 原版 WorldGen.beachDistance 是常量 380(cs:4102)
538	      let x = rng.int(200, w - 201);
539	      let y = rng.int(Math.floor(gs.worldSurfaceLow), Math.floor(gs.worldSurface) - 1);
540	      const oceanLevel = (gs.worldSurface + gs.rockLevel) / 2 + 40;
541	      while (y <= oceanLevel && (x < gs.beachDistance || x > w - gs.beachDistance)) {
542	        x = rng.int(300, w - 301);
543	        y = rng.int(Math.floor(gs.worldSurfaceLow), Math.floor(gs.worldSurface) - 1);
544	      }
545	      if (!st.inBounds(x, y)) continue;
546	      const i = st.idx(x, y);
547	      // cs:17260-17284 两分支：空格走墙门（2/59/244，墙 244 → 天空箱）；
548	      // 落点活性格走 101×101（步 2）蓄水池采样找墙 244 空格（Next(num8) 命中即换点）
549	      let sky = false;
550	      let cx = x, cy = y;
551	      if (!st.flags[i]) {
552	        if (st.wall[i] === 2 || st.wall[i] === 59 || st.wall[i] === 244) {
553	          if (st.wall[i] === 244) sky = true;
554	        } else {
555	          continue;   // flag3=false → 本轮作废
556	        }
557	      } else {
558	        let num8 = 1;
559	        let hit = false;
560	        for (let j = cx - 50; j <= cx + 50; j += 2) {
561	          for (let k = cy - 50; k <= cy + 50; k += 2) {
562	            if (k >= gs.worldSurface) continue;
563	            if (!st.inBounds(j, k) || st.flags[st.idx(j, k)]) continue;
564	            if (st.wall[st.idx(j, k)] !== 244) continue;
565	            if (rng.int(0, num8 - 1) !== 0) continue;                    // Next(num8)==0
566	            sky = true;
567	            num8++;
568	            hit = true;
569	            cx = j; cy = k;
570	          }
571	        }
572	        if (!hit) continue;   // 蓄水池无墙 244 空格 → 本轮作废
573	      }
574	      const flags: ChestFlags = sky ? { sky: true } : { wooden: true };
575	      if (placeBuriedChest(st, rng, gs, chests, cx, cy, { style: sky ? 12 : 0, flags })) ok = true;
576	    }
577	  }
578	}
579	
580	/** UnderwaterChests 的 9×w/4200 段（cs:17381-17453）：每下标两趟 while 各 1 箱——
581	 *  ① y∈[1, UnderworldLayer)（内层液体重掷域 [50, UnderworldLayer)）
582	 *  ② y∈[worldSurface, UnderworldLayer)。主件序：单次 Next(10)==0 → 863，
583	 *  否则计数器 186→4404→277→187（num4 每迭代无条件 ++，命中 863 也前进）。 */
584	export function runWaterChestsPass(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, gs: GenState, chests: ChestSink): void {
585	  const { w } = st;
586	  const num5 = w / 4200;
587	  const count = 9 * num5;
588	  let num4 = 0;
589	  for (let n = 0; n < count; n++) {
590	    num4++;
591	    let main: number;
592	    if (rng.int(0, 9) === 0) {                                          // Next(10)
593	      main = 863;
594	    } else {
595	      switch (num4) {
596	        case 1: main = 186; break;
597	        case 2: main = 4404; break;
598	        case 3: main = 277; break;
599	        default: main = 187; num4 = 0; break;
600	      }
601	    }
602	    // 第一趟（cs:17424-17431）：y 首掷 [1, h-200)，重掷 [50, h-200)
603	    for (let loop = 0; loop < 2; loop++) {
604	      const yLo = loop === 0 ? 1 : Math.floor(gs.worldSurface);
605	      let ok = false;
606	      for (let tries = 0; tries < 10000 && !ok; tries++) {
607	        let x = rng.int(50, w - 51);                                    // Next(50, w-50)
608	        let y = rng.int(yLo, st.h - 201);                               // Next(?, h-200)
609	        // cs:17425-17428：液体不足或非水 → 重掷（重掷 y 域换 [50, h-200)）。
610	        // liquidType 修 2026-08-12：本仓库 +1 编码(水=LIQUID_TYPE.WATER=1)——照抄原版
611	        // !==0(原版 Water=0) 会让水格恒不命中,拒绝采样死循环(世界生成卡"生物群系"根因)。
612	        // 守卫:100 万掷未命中 → 大声报错降级继续(防同类移植错位再变静默死循环)
613	        let __guard = 0;
614	        while (st.liquid[st.idx(x, y)] < 250 || st.liquidType[st.idx(x, y)] !== LIQUID_TYPE.WATER) {
615	          x = rng.int(50, w - 51);
616	          y = rng.int(50, st.h - 201);
617	          if (++__guard >= 1_000_000) {
618	            console.error('[worldgen] runWaterChestsPass 拒绝采样 100 万掷未命中水中格——疑似 liquidType/liquid 编码错位或本世界无合格水体,降级跳过本箱');
619	            break;
620	          }
621	        }
622	        if (__guard >= 1_000_000) continue;
623	        // cs:17427：trySlope 门 = 落点在海滩带内（x < 380 || x > w-380）
624	        if (placeBuriedChest(st, rng, gs, chests, x, y, {
625	          style: 17, loot: main, flags: { water: true },
626	          trySlope: x < gs.beachDistance || x > w - gs.beachDistance,
627	        })) ok = true;
628	      }
629	    }
630	  }
631	}
632	

```

116	function smoothSlope(st: TileStore, x: number, y: number): void {

121	  const flag3 = solidOrSlopedTile(st, x, y + 1);                          // 下方实心
122	  const flag4 = solidOrSlopedTile(st, x - 1, y);                          // 左实心
123	  const flag5 = solidOrSlopedTile(st, x + 1, y);                          // 右实心
124	  const num = ((flag ? 1 : 0) << 3) | ((flag3 ? 1 : 0) << 2) | ((flag4 ? 1 : 0) << 1) | (flag5 ? 1 : 0);
125	  switch (num) {
126	    case 10: if (!flag2) { st.half[i] = 0; st.slope[i] = 3; } break;
127	    case 9:  if (!flag2) { st.half[i] = 0; st.slope[i] = 4; } break;
128	    case 6:  st.half[i] = 0; st.slope[i] = 1; break;
129	    case 5:  st.half[i] = 0; st.slope[i] = 2; break;
130	    case 4:  st.slope[i] = 0; st.half[i] = 1; break;
131	    default: st.half[i] = 0; st.slope[i] = 0; break;

281	/** 原版 pass "Waterfalls"（WorldGen.cs:7697-7743）：水边唇缘砸半砖（瀑布触发的来源） */
282	export function runWaterfallLipPass(st: TileStore, _rng: RNG, _gs: GenState): void {
283	  const { w, h } = st;
284	  // —— 第一遍（L7701-7723）：实心顶格、左右空、侧旁有水 → 砸半砖 ——
285	  for (let i = 20; i < w - 20; i++) {
286	    for (let j = 20; j < h - 20; j++) {
287	      if (!solidTile(st, i, j)) continue;
288	      const li = st.idx(i - 1, j), ri = st.idx(i + 1, j);
289	      // cs:16709:左右邻须 !active(曾误用 type!==0 → 幽灵格侧邻漏砸半砖)
290	      if (st.flags[li] !== 0 || st.flags[ri] !== 0) continue;
291	      if (!solidTile(st, i, j + 1)) continue;
292	      if (st.liquid[li] === 0 && st.liquid[ri] === 0) continue;
293	      // 上方 8-20 格内已有半砖则跳过（原版防连续砸穿）
294	      let flag = true;
295	      const num3 = 8 + Math.floor(_rng.next() * 12), num4 = 8 + Math.floor(_rng.next() * 12);
296	      for (let k = j - num3; k <= j + num4; k++) {
297	        if (k >= 0 && k < h && st.half[st.idx(i, k)]) flag = false;
298	      }
299	      if (flag) poundTile(st, i, j);
300	    }
301	  }
302	  // —— 第二遍（L7725-7741）：唇缘向相邻实心格扩展半砖 ——
303	  for (let i = 20; i < w - 20; i++) {
304	    for (let j = 20; j < h - 20; j++) {
305	      const ti = st.idx(i, j);
306	      if (!solidTile(st, i, j) || !solidTile(st, i, j + 1)) continue;
307	      if (!solidTile(st, i + 1, j) && st.half[st.idx(i - 1, j)] && st.liquid[st.idx(i - 2, j)] > 0) {
308	        poundTile(st, i, j);
309	      }
310	      if (!solidTile(st, i - 1, j) && st.half[st.idx(i + 1, j)] && st.liquid[st.idx(i + 2, j)] > 0) {
311	        poundTile(st, i, j);
312	      }
313	    }
314	  }
315	}

1409	  //      type=1 等），旧 framed tile 帧残留进新类型 = 渲染越界/错位（实测 sheet1
1410	  //      fx=1080 / sheet4 fx=252 / sheet51 fy=216 等）。像素级粗判（超出表宽/高即清 0），
1411	  //      非均匀行高表（187 的 16/18 行）不受影响（越表判比步长判宽松）
1412	  {
1413	    const wmax = new Map<number, number>();
1414	    const hmax = new Map<number, number>();
1415	    for (let i = 0; i < store.type.length; i++) {
1416	      if (!store.flags[i]) continue;
1417	      const d = TILE_DEFS[store.type[i]];
1418	      const sh = d?.vanilla?.sheet;
1419	      if (sh == null) continue;
1420	      let w = wmax.get(sh), h2 = hmax.get(sh);
1421	      if (w === undefined || h2 === undefined) {
1422	        const m = V_SHEET_DIM[sh];
1423	        w = m ? m[0] : 0; h2 = m ? m[1] : 0;
1424	        wmax.set(sh, w); hmax.set(sh, h2);
1425	      }
1426	      if (w === 0) continue;
1427	      // ★ 回绕区间（≥32768）是【刻意的有符号偏移】——棕榈 323 干身/树冠的
1428	      // frameY=倾斜像素（65534=-2 等，VanillaTiler.ts palm 分支同约定解码），
1429	      // 不是格位索引，绝不可清（左倾棕榈被清 → 树冠+倾斜全部消失）。
1430	      // 残留脏帧只会是小正值，只对正值做越表判定
1431	      const fxv = store.frameX[i], fyv = store.frameY[i];
1432	      if (fxv < 32768 && fyv < 32768 && (fxv >= w || fyv >= h2)) {
1433	        store.frameX[i] = 0; store.frameY[i] = 0;
1434	      }
1435	    }
1436	  }
1437	  // ---- 出生点（原版 SpawnPoint cs:19605-19724）已归位为独立管线槽「出生点」
1438	  //      （地表装饰之后、草墙之前——注册序 Piles(18900) < SpawnPoint(19605) <
1439	  //      GrassWall(19734)）——见 spawnPointPass；曾内嵌本函数尾（全部 pass 之后
1440	  //      执行）,2026-08-13 清零批归位。finalize 仅保留幽灵/帧净化与常量同步
1441	
1442	  // 原版管线的地表/岩石线来自 TerrainPass 输出,旧 fbm 常数仅作回退
1443	  const gs = ctxGs[0];
1444	  if (gs) {
1445	    world.groundLevel = gs.worldSurface;
1446	    world.rockLevel = gs.rockLevel;
1447	  } else {
1448	    world.groundLevel = Math.floor(store.h * 0.28);
1449	    world.rockLevel = Math.floor(store.h * 0.33);
1450	  }
1451	  // 原版树样式（RandomizeTreeStyle 掷出：边界 + 互异样式）
1452	  if (gs) {
1453	    world.treeX = gs.treeX;
1454	    world.treeStyle = gs.treeStyle;
1455	    world.trees = gs.genTrees; // 砍伐整棵判定（Player/Game 消费）
1456	  }
1457	  // 树登记保留:树干不再被清理盒删除,world.trees 与实际树一一对应
1458	  // (原 |t.x-sx|<=5 过滤是配合"清掉树干防幽灵树"的,已不适用;
1459	  //  上空清理盒(3×3+5×5)已随出生点段迁往 spawnPointPass)
1460	}
1461	
1462	// ---------- 出生点（原版 SpawnPoint pass，注册 cs:19605 / 实现 cs:19605-19724） ----------
1463	// 中心随机扩张采样自然地表（不改地形），叠加我们的上空净空清理盒。
1464	// 旧实现为"找最平坦窗口+人工整平平台"——出生点是一块突兀平台，与原版自然地表不符。
1465	// 2026-08-13 前内嵌 finalize 尾（全部 pass 之后执行）——现按注册序归位为独立槽
1466	// （「地表装饰」之后、「草墙」之前）。
1467	function spawnPointPass({ store, rng, world }: GenCtx) {
1468	  let range = 5;
1469	  let sx = Math.floor(store.w / 2), sy = 0;
1470	  for (;;) {
1471	    const x = Math.floor(store.w / 2) + rng.int(-range, range); // Next(-num2, num2+1)
1472	    for (let y = 0; y < store.h; y++) {
1473	      const i = store.idx(x, y);
1474	      // 首个实心格（原版 cs:19683 仅 active && type!=379——379 是非实心装饰格，
1475	      // 实心判据天然跳过；solid 判据防树干致浮空出生，保留）
1476	      if (store.flags[i] && !!TILE_DEFS[store.type[i]]?.solid && store.type[i] !== TILE_379) { sx = x; sy = y; break; }
1477	    }
1478	    let bad = false;
1479	    range++;
1480	    const gs2 = ctxGs[0];
1481	    if (gs2 && sy > gs2.worldSurface) bad = true;
1482	    // 出生点上方 3 格任一有水即弃（原版 cs:19700-19703 查 sy-1/-2/-3 三格——
1483	    // 只查 sy-1 会漏检水面下 1-2 格,出生进浅水）
1484	    for (const dy of [1, 2, 3]) {
1485	      if (store.liquid[store.idx(sx, Math.max(0, sy - dy))] > 0) { bad = true; break; }
1486	    }
1487	    // IsTileNearby(379, 10)（cs:19704-19707）：±10 盒内有 379 → 重试
1488	    if (!bad) {
1489	      outer:
1490	      for (let nx = sx - 10; nx <= sx + 10; nx++) {
1491	        for (let ny = sy - 10; ny <= sy + 10; ny++) {
1492	          if (store.inBounds(nx, ny) && store.flags[store.idx(nx, ny)] && store.type[store.idx(nx, ny)] === TILE_379) {
1493	            bad = true;
1494	            break outer;
1495	          }
1496	        }
1497	      }
1498	    }
1499	    if (!bad) break;
